\displaystyle+\sum_{m=2}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1},

from which (
3.35
) follows.

Setting
z_{1}=z_{2}=z,t_{1}=t_{2}=t>0
, (
3.35
) simplifies to

(3.36)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\|f_{n}(\cdot;z,t)\|_{n}^{2}\leq\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{n}\left[2U\left(\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t}}{z/t}{\frac{z}{t}}{\frac{z}{t}}\right)+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}\right].

We proceed to prove (
3.34
).
For every
z,t>0
, choose
\varepsilon>0
such that
\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}<1
.
By (
3.36
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\|f_{n}(\cdot;z,t)\|_{n}^{2}=u_{0}(z,t)^{2}+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\|f_{n}(\cdot;z,t)\|_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq u_{0}(z,t)^{2}+U\left(\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t}}{z/t}{\frac{z}{t}}{\frac{z}{t}}\right)\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}}{2^{n-1}}}{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}/2^{n-1}}{\frac{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}}{2^{n-1}}}{\frac{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}}{2^{n-1}}}+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{n}\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}.

By the choice of
\varepsilon
, we have
\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}<1
and hence

\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}^{n}F_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{2^{n-1}}}{\Gamma_{t}^{n}F_{\varepsilon}^{n}/2^{n-1}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}^{n}F_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{2^{n-1}}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}^{n}F_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{2^{n-1}}}=\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{1-\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/1-\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{1-\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{1-\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)}}=\mathchoice{\frac{2\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2-\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}}{2\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2-\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2-\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2-\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}}<\infty.

It remains to show the last series converges.
First, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k},

from which it follows that

\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}\leq\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}n\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k},

and so

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{n}

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{n-1}n\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{k!}}{1/k!}{\frac{1}{k!}}{\frac{1}{k!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{n}(n+1)\sum_{k=0}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{k!}}{1/k!}{\frac{1}{k!}}{\frac{1}{k!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{n}(n+1)\sum_{k=0}^{n}2^{n}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{k!}}{1/k!}{\frac{1}{k!}}{\frac{1}{k!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)te^{\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon})^{n}(n+1)

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t}{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}-1)^{2}}}{\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t/(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}-1)^{2}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t}{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}-1)^{2}}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}f(\varepsilon)t}{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}-1)^{2}}}e^{\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}},

where convergence in the last equality follows by the fact that
\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}<1
.
Hence for all
z,t>0
we have (
3.34
) and so the series in (
3.4
) converges to
u(z,t)
in
L^{2}(\Omega)
.
The rest of the theorem follows similarly as in the proof of Theorem
3.1
for the case of space-time white noise.
∎

3.2.
Ratio Comparison in
L^{2}

With existence and uniqueness established, next we study the behavior of the solution near the boundary of the domain.
As in (3.10) in Lemma 3.4 of
[
14
]
, it is insightful to study how the solution to the Kimura equation with a potential compares to the unperturbed solution.
In our setting the solution
u
is a random field, so instead of a point-wise comparison, we examine the ratio between
u(z,t)
and the unperturbed, deterministic solution
u_{0}(z,t)
in
L^{2}(\Omega)
.
Specifically, recalling that
u_{n}(z,t):=\mathcal{I}_{n}(f_{n}(\cdot;z,t))
with
f_{n}
as defined in (
3.5
), we study

(3.37)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u_{n}(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]

for
z
near 0 and
t
small.

In the case of (
1.6
) with space-time white noise, we see that the solution is quantifiably different at the boundary.
Indeed, using the fact that
I_{1}(w)\geq w/2
for every
w>0
, by the definition of
\mathbb{E}[u_{1}^{2}(z,t)]
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}(z,t)\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-r)(1-e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{w}{r}}{w/r}{\frac{w}{r}}{\frac{w}{r}}})^{2}\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle\geq z^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{(t-r)^{4}}}{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}/(t-r)^{4}}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{(t-r)^{4}}}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{(t-r)^{4}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-r}}{2w/t-r}{\frac{2w}{t-r}}{\frac{2w}{t-r}}}(1-e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{w}{r}}{w/r}{\frac{w}{r}}{\frac{w}{r}}})^{2}\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}r,

and so by direction computations it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}^{2}(z,t)\right]

\displaystyle=z^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{2t(t-r)(t+r)}}{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}/2t(t-r)(t+r)}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{2t(t-r)(t+r)}}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{2t(t-r)(t+r)}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\geq\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{4t^{2}}}{z^{2}/4t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{4t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{4t^{2}}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{(t-r)}}{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}/(t-r)}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{(t-r)}}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{2z/t-r}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}{\frac{2z}{t-r}}}}{(t-r)}}\mathrm{d}r=\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{4t^{2}}}{z^{2}/4t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{4t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{4t^{2}}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t}}{2z/t}{\frac{2z}{t}}{\frac{2z}{t}}}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}{e^{-\tau}/\tau}{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}\mathrm{d}\tau.

Since
1-e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t}}{z/t}{\frac{z}{t}}{\frac{z}{t}}}\approx z/t
for fixed
t
and sufficiently small
z
, we in turn have

\displaystyle\lim_{z\searrow 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u_{1}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u_{1}(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{1}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{1}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\geq\lim_{z\searrow 0}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t}}{2z/t}{\frac{2z}{t}}{\frac{2z}{t}}}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}{e^{-\tau}/\tau}{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}\mathrm{d}\tau=\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}{e^{-\tau}/\tau}{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}{\frac{e^{-\tau}}{\tau}}\mathrm{d}\tau=\infty,

where the last integral diverges as it is the limit of the gamma function at zero.

To remedy this, we introduce a degenerate coefficient of order
\beta>0
to
\dot{W}
and consider the following stochastic Kimura equation:

\begin{cases}&\partial_{t}u\left(z,t\right)=z\partial_{z}^{2}u\left(z,t\right)+\hat{z}^{\beta}u\left(z,t\right)\dot{W}\left(z,t\right)\quad\quad z,t>0,\\
&\hskip 9.10486ptu(z,0)=u_{0}(z)\hfill z>0,\\
&\hskip 10.81204ptu(0,t)=0\hfill t>0,\end{cases}

where recall that
\hat{z}:=z\wedge 1
.
It can be shown similarly to
\mathsection\ref{sec:eu}
that (
3.1
) admits a unique mild solution.
In addition, the second moments of the solution can be recursively computed by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u_{n}^{2}(z,t)]=\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\hskip-14.22636pt

\displaystyle\gamma(r-s)\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\hskip-8.53581ptf(x-y)\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}

(3.38)

\displaystyle\cdot q_{0}(z,x,t-r)q_{0}(z,y,t-s)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}(x,r)u_{n-1}(y,s)]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s.

In the following subsections, we show that so long as
\beta>0
, the stochastic and deterministic solutions are “comparable” in a proper sense.
As before, we begin our analysis in the case of space-time white noise which we bootstrap to the case of colored noise.

3.2.1.
Space-Time White Noise

Our strategy for the case of space-time white noise is similar to the one used to show convergence of (
3.4
) in
L^{2}(\Omega)
.
Namely, we use (
3.8
) to recursively compute a bound with some repetitive pattern.
We first make use of the following technical lemma for
n=1
.

Lemma 3.5
.

For
\beta,z,t-\tau,\tau>0
, define

(3.39)

K(z,\tau;2\beta):=\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}\mathrm{d}w.

Then, there exists
C:=C_{\beta}>0
, such that for all
z,t,t-\tau,\tau>0
,

(3.40)

K(z,\tau;2\beta)\leq\mathchoice{\frac{4C}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{4C/(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}{\frac{4C}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{\frac{4C}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha},

whenever
0<\alpha\leq 2\beta\wedge 1/2
.

Proof.

We further split
K(z,\tau;2\beta)
into two terms:

\displaystyle K_{1}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}\mathrm{d}w,

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle:=\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}\mathrm{d}w.

We will treat
K_{1}
and
K_{2}
in two separate cases.

Case
\tau\leq t/2
.

In this case, we apply Proposition
2.2
and (
3.7
) to bound
K_{1}
as

\displaystyle K_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}{z^{2}/(t-\tau)^{4}}{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}\int_{0}^{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}\hat{w}^{2\beta}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/(t-\tau)}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}}\mathrm{d}w}{\displaystyle e^{-2}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{z^{2}/t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}}}{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}{z^{2}/(t-\tau)^{4}}{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}\int_{0}^{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}\hat{w}^{2\beta}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/(t-\tau)}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}}\mathrm{d}w/\displaystyle e^{-2}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{z^{2}/t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}}{\frac{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}{z^{2}/(t-\tau)^{4}}{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{(t-\tau)^{4}}}\int_{0}^{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}\hat{w}^{2\beta}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/(t-\tau)}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{(t-\tau)}}}\mathrm{d}w}{\displaystyle \displaystyle\left((t-\tau)^{-3}\leq z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}u^{-3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}(t-\tau)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{1/\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}\int_{0}^{\mathchoice{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{t-\tau/\sqrt{z}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}}\hat{u}^{4\beta}u^{2}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}\mathrm{d}u}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{2C}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}{2C/\sqrt{t-\tau}}{\frac{2C}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}{\frac{2C}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}\hat{z}^{2\beta}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 2C\hat{z}^{2\beta}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{-\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{z^{-\alpha}/(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}{\frac{z^{-\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{\frac{z^{-\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}\leq 2C\mathchoice{\frac{\hat{z}^{2\beta-\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{\hat{z}^{2\beta-\alpha}/(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}{\frac{\hat{z}^{2\beta-\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{\frac{\hat{z}^{2\beta-\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}.

Similarly as above, for
K_{2}
we apply (
2.19
) from Proposition
2.2
and (
3.7
) to get

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\sqrt{z}/t-\tau}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}}u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)\mathrm{d}w}{\displaystyle e^{-2}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{z^{2}/t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}}}{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\sqrt{z}/t-\tau}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}}u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)\mathrm{d}w/\displaystyle e^{-2}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{z^{2}/t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}}{\frac{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\sqrt{z}/t-\tau}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}}u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)\mathrm{d}w}{\displaystyle e^{-2}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{z^{2}/t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}}}{\frac{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\sqrt{z}/t-\tau}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{\sqrt{z}}{t-\tau}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}}u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)\mathrm{d}w}{\displaystyle e^{-2}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{z^{2}/t^{2}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}{\frac{z^{2}}{t^{2}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}t^{2}}{t-\tau}}{z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}t^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}t^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}t^{2}}{t-\tau}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\tau^{2}}}{\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/\tau^{2}}{\frac{\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\tau^{2}}}{\frac{\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\tau^{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}}\mathrm{d}w

\displaystyle\left(\tau>\mathchoice{\frac{t}{2}}{t/2}{\frac{t}{2}}{\frac{t}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}/t-\tau}{\frac{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{(t-\tau)^{2}/z}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}{\frac{(t-\tau)^{2}}{z}}}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-\sqrt{w})^{2}}{t-\tau}}}\mathrm{d}w

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}/\sqrt{t-\tau}}{\frac{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}{\frac{Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{1/\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{t-\tau/\sqrt{z}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}}^{\infty}\hat{u}^{4\beta}u^{2}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}}\mathrm{d}u\right).

Furthermore, if
z>t-\tau
then
K_{2}\leq 2C(t-\tau)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\hat{z}^{2\beta}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq 2C\hat{z}^{2\beta-\alpha}(t-\tau)^{-1+\alpha}
.
Meanwhile, if
z\leq t-\tau
we get

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle\leq Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}(t-\tau)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{7}{2}}{7/2}{\frac{7}{2}}{\frac{7}{2}}}\cdot(t-\tau)^{3}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{1/\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{t-\tau/\sqrt{z}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}}^{\infty}u^{4\beta+2}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}}\mathrm{d}u\right)

\displaystyle\left((t-\tau)^{3}\leq z^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}u^{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C(t-\tau)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{7}{2}}{7/2}{\frac{7}{2}}{\frac{7}{2}}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{1/\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi(t-\tau)}}}\int_{\mathchoice{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{t-\tau/\sqrt{z}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}{\frac{t-\tau}{\sqrt{z}}}}^{\infty}u^{4\beta+5}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}/t-\tau}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2(\sqrt{z}-u)^{2}}{t-\tau}}}\mathrm{d}u\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2C(t-\tau)^{2\beta-1}.

Combining all these cases, we have shown that

K(z,\tau;2\beta)=K_{1}+K_{2}\leq 4C(t-\tau)^{\alpha-1}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}.

∎

Note that if
\beta\leq\beta^{\prime}
, then for every
w>0
,
\hat{w}^{2\beta^{\prime}}\leq\hat{w}^{2\beta}
and hence

(3.41)

K(z,\tau;2\beta^{\prime})\leq K(z,\tau;2\beta).

Remark 3.2
.

We observe that for
z,t>0
and
\beta>0
, if we define

(3.42)

\tilde{K}(z,\tau;\beta):=\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{\beta}q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}(w,\tau)}}{u_{0}(w,\tau)/u_{0}(w,\tau)}{\frac{u_{0}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}(w,\tau)}}{\frac{u_{0}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}(w,\tau)}}\mathrm{d}w,

then following the proof of Lemma 3.5 almost step-by-step (with minor changes), we can get that

(3.43)

\tilde{K}(z,\tau;\beta)\leq 4C(\hat{z}\vee(t-\tau))^{\beta}.

This observation on
\tilde{K}(z,\tau;\beta)
will be useful in
\mathsection\ref{ssec:ratioColored}
in treating the ratio
u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)
under colored noise.

From this, the ratio follows straightforwardly by computing the integral in time.

Corollary 3.1
.

Let
\beta,C,
and
\alpha
be the same as above and fixed.
Set
C_{\alpha,\beta}:=4C/\alpha
.
Then for all
z,t>0
,

(3.44)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u_{1}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u_{1}(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{1}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{1}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}.

Proof.

Follows directly by integrating the result from Lemma
3.5
in
\tau
over
[0,t]
.
∎

In light of Corollary
3.1
, for every
w,\tau>0
,

\mathbb{E}[u_{1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\tau^{\alpha}(\hat{w}\vee\tau)^{2\beta-\alpha}u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau),

and hence it becomes possible for us to re-use the bound for the function
K(z,\tau;\cdot)
in Lemma
3.5
.
As it turns out, this is the key ingredient for the main result for this section.

Theorem 3.3
.

Let
\beta>0
.
When
W
is a space-time white noise, there exists
T\in(0,1)
such that for every
t\in[0,T]
, there exists
C_{t,\beta}>0
such that

(3.45)

\sup_{z>0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]<C_{t,\beta},

where
T
and
C_{t,\beta}
can be explicitly determined.
Furthermore, for every fixed
z>0
,

(3.46)

\lim_{t\searrow 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}-1\right)^{2}\right]=0.

Proof.

In light of (
3.37
) we proceed by induction on
n\geq 1
to show that for
\beta,z,t>0
and
0<\alpha\leq 2\beta\wedge 1/2
,

(3.47)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u_{n}(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}t^{n\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{(2\beta-\alpha)}.

The base case is given by Corollary
3.1
.
Next, assume that (
3.47
) holds for
n-1
with
n\geq 2
.
Then by using (
3.47
) and (
3.41
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u_{n}(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{0}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\right)\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{0}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\right)\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{0}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\right)\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{\int_{0}^{t}\left(\int_{0}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\right)\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n-1}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{(n-1)\alpha+2\beta-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle+C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n-1}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{(n-1)\alpha}\int_{\tau}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{4\beta-\alpha}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)/u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{0}^{2}(w,\tau)}{u_{0}^{2}(z,t)}}\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n-1}\int_{0}^{t}\tau^{(n-1)\alpha}K(z,\tau;2\beta)\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq 4CC_{\alpha,\beta}^{n-1}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{\tau^{(n-1)\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{\tau^{(n-1)\alpha}/(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}{\frac{\tau^{(n-1)\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}{\frac{\tau^{(n-1)\alpha}}{(t-\tau)^{1-\alpha}}}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}t^{n\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha},

as desired.
Hence (
3.47
) holds for
n\geq 1
.
Therefore, whenever
C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}<1
, by substituting (
3.47
) into (
3.37
),

(3.48)

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}t^{n\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}=\mathchoice{\frac{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}/1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}{\frac{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\frac{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}.

We continue to prove (
3.45
) and (
3.46
).
Let
\alpha=2\beta\wedge 1/2
and
T>0
such that
C_{\alpha,\beta}T^{\alpha}<1
.
From (
3.48
) it follows that for every
t\in[0,T]
,

\sup_{z>0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\mathchoice{\frac{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}/1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}{\frac{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\frac{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}=:C_{t,\beta},

which is precisely (
3.45
).
On the other hand, for any
z>0
fixed,

\lim_{t\searrow 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}-1\right)^{2}\right]=\lim_{t\searrow 0}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u_{n}(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u_{n}(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\lim_{t\searrow 0}\mathchoice{\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}/1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}{\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\frac{C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}=0,

from which (
3.46
) follows.
∎

3.2.2.
Colored Noise

As before, we seek to bootstrap the results from the case of space-time white noise.
Fortunately, the same techniques we developed in
\mathsection\ref{sec:eu}
are still applicable to us now.
Once again, we build to the main result, Theorem
3.4
, by first treating the case
n=1
.

Lemma 3.6
.

Let
z_{1},z_{2},t_{1},t_{2}>0
and let
\beta,\varepsilon>0
, then

(3.49)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\mathbb{E}\left[\mathchoice{\frac{u_{1}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{1}(z_{2},t_{2})}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{u_{1}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{1}(z_{2},t_{2})/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{u_{1}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{1}(z_{2},t_{2})}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{u_{1}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{1}(z_{2},t_{2})}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}\right]\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{t_{1}^{\alpha}t_{2}^{\alpha}(\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})^{2\beta-\alpha}(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})^{2\beta-\alpha}},

where
0<\alpha\leq 2\beta\wedge 1/2
and
C_{\alpha,\beta}
may be greater than in Corollary
3.1
.

Proof.

We define

\displaystyle V_{1}

\displaystyle:=\int_{[0,t_{1}]\times[0,t_{2}]}\hskip-36.98857pt\gamma(r-s)\int_{A_{\varepsilon}}\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}f(x-y)q_{0}(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r)q_{0}(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle V_{2}

\displaystyle:=\int_{[0,t_{1}]\times[0,t_{2}]}\hskip-36.98857pt\gamma(r-s)\int_{A_{\varepsilon}^{c}}\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}f(x-y)q_{0}(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r)q_{0}(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s)\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s,

and treat each term separately.
First, applying Proposition
5.2
and Lemma
3.5
, we see

\displaystyle V_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{t_{1}}K(z_{1},r,2\beta)\mathrm{d}r\int_{0}^{t_{2}}K(z_{2},s,2\beta)\mathrm{d}r}

(3.50)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\varepsilon}C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{t_{1}^{\alpha}t_{2}^{\alpha}(\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})^{2\beta-\alpha}(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})^{2\beta-\alpha}},

for
K
as in (
3.39
).
For the second term, by Proposition
5.2
and Remark
3.2
, we see

\displaystyle V_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\tilde{K}(z_{1},r,\beta)^{2}\mathrm{d}r\int_{0}^{t_{2}}\tilde{K}(z_{2},s,\beta)^{2}\mathrm{d}s}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}f(\varepsilon)(4C)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}(\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})^{\beta}(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})^{\beta}

(3.51)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}f(\varepsilon)C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{t_{1}^{\alpha}t_{2}^{\alpha}(\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})^{2\beta-\alpha}(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})^{2\beta-\alpha}},

where (
3.51
) follows because
\hat{z}_{1},\hat{z}_{2},t_{1},t_{2}<1
.
Combining (
3.50
) and (
3.51
) in turn leads to (
3.49
).
∎

Considering the form of (
3.47
), it is not hard to see that for
n\geq 1
, we expect

(3.52)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\mathchoice{\frac{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}^{n}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))^{n}C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}(t_{1}t_{2})^{\mathchoice{\frac{n\alpha}{2}}{n\alpha/2}{\frac{n\alpha}{2}}{\frac{n\alpha}{2}}}((\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2}))^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\alpha/2}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}.

Before presenting the proof, we first recall Remark
3.1
, which motivated the need for a bound better than geometric in case one could not guarantee the existence of
\varepsilon
such that the concerned series in
\mathsection\ref{ssec:euColored}
is convergent for all time
t
.
However, the mere issue of convergence will not occur here by virtue of only studying the solution
u
for small values of
t
.
Given any value of
\varepsilon>0
, we may assume that either
t_{1}
or
t_{2}
is sufficiently small as to ensure summability of (
3.52
) over
n\geq 1
.

Theorem 3.4
.

Let
\beta>0
and
\varepsilon>0
.
Suppose
f
satisfies conditions (
i
) and (
ii
).
Then there exists
T\in(0,1)
such that for every
t\in[0,T]
, there exists
C_{t,\beta,\epsilon}>0
such that

(3.53)

\sup_{z>0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]<C_{t,\beta,\varepsilon},

where
T
and
C_{t,\beta,\epsilon}
can be explicitly determined.
Furthermore, for every fixed
z>0
,

(3.54)

\lim_{t\searrow 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}-1\right)^{2}\right]=0.

Proof.

We begin by proving (
3.52
) by induction on
n\geq 1
.
The base case is given by Lemma
3.6
.
Now assume that (
3.52
) holds for
n-1
with
n\geq 2
.
Define

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\int_{[0,t_{1}]\times[0,t_{2}]}\hskip-36.98857pt\gamma(r-s)\int_{A_{\varepsilon}}\hskip-8.53581pt\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}f(x-y)q_{0}(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r)q_{0}(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07164pt\cdot(\hat{x}\vee r)^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\alpha/2}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}(\hat{y}\vee s)^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\alpha/2}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\int_{[0,t_{1}]\times[0,t_{2}]}\hskip-36.98857pt\gamma(r-s)\int_{A_{\varepsilon}^{c}}\hskip-8.53581pt\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}f(x-y)q_{0}(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r)q_{0}(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07164pt\cdot(\hat{x}\vee r)^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\alpha/2}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}(\hat{y}\vee s)^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\alpha/2}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\mathchoice{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{u_{0}(x,r)u_{0}(y,s)}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s.

We start by examining
\tilde{V}_{1}
.
Applying Proposition
5.2
and (
3.41
), then following the proof for (
3.50
), we see

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{t_{1}}K(z_{1},r,2\beta)\mathrm{d}r\int_{0}^{t_{2}}K(z_{2},s,2\beta)\mathrm{d}s}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\varepsilon}C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{t_{1}^{\alpha}t_{2}^{\alpha}(\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})^{2\beta-\alpha}(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})^{2\beta-\alpha}}.

Likewise, again by Proposition
5.2
and (
3.41
), then following the proof for (
3.51
),

\tilde{V}_{2}\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}f(\varepsilon)C_{\alpha,\beta}\sqrt{t_{1}^{\alpha}t_{2}^{\alpha}(\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})^{2\beta-\alpha}(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})^{2\beta-\alpha}}.

Putting it all together with the inductive assumption, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]/u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}{\frac{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}{\frac{\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{n}(z_{2},t_{2})]}{u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})}}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}^{n-1}\left(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon)\right)^{n-1}C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n-1}(t_{1}t_{2})^{\mathchoice{\frac{(n-1)\alpha}{2}}{(n-1)\alpha/2}{\frac{(n-1)\alpha}{2}}{\frac{(n-1)\alpha}{2}}}\left(\tilde{V}_{1}+\tilde{V}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}^{n}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))^{n}C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n\alpha}{2}}{n\alpha/2}{\frac{n\alpha}{2}}{\frac{n\alpha}{2}}}\left((\hat{z}_{1}\vee t_{1})(\hat{z}_{2}\vee t_{2})\right)^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\alpha/2}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}{\frac{\alpha}{2}}},

as claimed.

Next, given
\varepsilon
and
\alpha
, choose
t>0
such that
\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}<1
.
Then by (
3.37
) and (
3.52
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Gamma_{t}^{n}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))^{n}C_{\alpha,\beta}^{n}t^{n\alpha}(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha},

(3.55)

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}/1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}{\frac{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\frac{(\hat{z}\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}.

We continue to prove (
3.53
) and (
3.54
).
First, let
\alpha=2\beta\wedge 1/2
and
T>0
be such that
\Gamma_{T}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}T^{\alpha}<1
. For all
t\in[0,T]
, from (
3.55
) it follows that

\sup_{z>0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\mathchoice{\frac{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}/1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}{\frac{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\frac{(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}=:C_{t,\beta,\varepsilon},

which is precisely (
3.53
).
On the other hand, for any
z>0
fixed,

\lim_{t\searrow 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathchoice{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{u(z,t)/u_{0}(z,t)}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}{\frac{u(z,t)}{u_{0}(z,t)}}-1\right)^{2}\right]\leq\lim_{t\searrow 0}\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}/1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}{\frac{\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}(1\vee t)^{2\beta-\alpha}}{1-\Gamma_{t}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))C_{\alpha,\beta}t^{\alpha}}}=0,

from which (
3.54
) follows.
∎

3.3.
L^{p}-
Bounds

In
\mathsection\ref{sec:eu}
, we presented the bounds (
3.12
) and (
3.14
), and (
3.36
) and (
3.34
) for the
L^{2}-
norms of
u_{n}(z,t)
and
u(z,t)
in the space-time white noise and colored noise cases, respectively.
Naturally, one might ask about bounds for general
L^{p}-
norms with
p\geq 2
.
However, unlike for the space
L^{2}(\Omega)
, there is no isometry or expansion property that allows us to directly treat
higher moments.
Nevertheless, for a solution
u
to either (
1.6
) or (
3.1
) with Wiener chaos expansion given by (
3.4
), (
2.5
) allows us to return to the treatment of the
L^{2}
-norm at the cost of an additional geometric term depending on
p
.

Unfortunately, while the methods developed in
\mathsection\ref{sec:eu}
give
u_{n}(z,t)\in L^{p}(\Omega)
for all
z,t>0
and
p\geq 2
, they do not produce sufficiently sharp bounds to guarantee convergence of the series in (
3.4
) to
u(z,t)
in
L^{p}(\Omega)
for
p\geq 2
.
For example, in the case of space-time white noise, directly applying the bound (
3.12
) with (
2.5
) and (
3.4
) gives

(3.56)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[|u_{n}(z,t)|^{p}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}\leq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{p-1}{2}}{p-1/2}{\frac{p-1}{2}}{\frac{p-1}{2}}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}},

for
z,t>0
.
Notice that the right-hand side of (
3.56
) fails to converge for
p\geq 3
.
As it turns out, the condition
\beta\geq 1/4
is sufficient to obtain results on
L^{p}
-integrability for all
p\geq 2
.
To this end, we need to refine the bounds developed in
\mathsection\ref{sec:eu}
.

3.3.1.
Space-Time White Noise

We begin by re-examining our bound for
\mathbb{E}[u_{1}^{2}(z,t)]
in the case of space-time white noise.
That is, recall that by Lemma
3.2
we have

\mathbb{E}[u_{1}^{2}(z,t)]\leq U\left(\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t}}{z/t}{\frac{z}{t}}{\frac{z}{t}}\right)=e^{\mathchoice{\frac{-z}{t}}{-z/t}{\frac{-z}{t}}{\frac{-z}{t}}}I_{0}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t}}{z/t}{\frac{z}{t}}{\frac{z}{t}}\right)-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t}}{2z/t}{\frac{2z}{t}}{\frac{2z}{t}}}\leq\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}.

While we uniformly bounded the function
U
for convenience, notice that we in fact have
U(x)=O(x^{-1/2})
.
Thus, it is possible to obtain a tighter estimate of
U
for large argument values by opting to not bound it by a constant.
We make this notion precise in our first refined result.

Lemma 3.7
.

There exists a constant
C>0
such that for all
z,t-\tau,\tau>0
,

(3.57)

\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)dw\leq Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(t-\tau\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Moreover, for
z,t>0

(3.58)

\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau\leq\mathchoice{\frac{C\sqrt{\pi}}{\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}\right)}}{C\sqrt{\pi}/\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}\right)}{\frac{C\sqrt{\pi}}{\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}\right)}}{\frac{C\sqrt{\pi}}{\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}\right)}}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}t^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}},

where
\Gamma
denotes the Gamma function.

Proof.

As in the proof of Lemma
3.2
, we have

(3.59)

\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)dw=\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}}{2z/\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}{\frac{2z}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}}{\frac{2z}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{2z/t-\tau}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}u^{-1}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t-\tau\right)u^{2}}{2z}}{\left(t-\tau\right)u^{2}/2z}{\frac{\left(t-\tau\right)u^{2}}{2z}}{\frac{\left(t-\tau\right)u^{2}}{2z}}}I_{1}^{2}\left(u\right)du.

In fact, the integral in the right-hand side of (
3.59
) is a representation of the Confluent Hypergeometric function
{}_{2}F_{2}
with parameters
a_{1}=3/2,a_{2}=1
and
b_{1}=2,b_{2}=3
(see
\mathsection\ref{sec:appendix}
).
The tail of said function is well-studied; using Proposition
5.4
, there exists a constant
C>0
depending only on
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
such that

\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)dw=\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}}{2z/\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}{\frac{2z}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}}{\frac{2z}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{2}}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{2z/t-\tau}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}}\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{z/t-\tau}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{}_{2}F_{2}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}},1\\
2,3\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Bigg|}\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{2z/t-\tau}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}\right]\leq Cz^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(t-\tau\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Hence we have (
3.57
).
Simply integrating with respect to
\tau
then gives (
3.58
).
∎

Now consider the stochastic Kimura equation (
3.1
) studied in
\mathsection\ref{sec:ratio}
.
With a little more effort, we can refine the estimate for
\mathbb{E}[u_{n}^{2}(z,t)]
by using Lemma
3.7
with the recursive expression (
3.38
) in the case of space-white noise.
As a result, we can continue to show convergence of the series in (
3.4
) to
u(z,t)
in
L^{p}(\Omega)
for all
p\geq 2
.

Theorem 3.5
.

For every
n\geq 1
,
p\geq 2
,
\beta\geq 1/4
, and
z,t>0
, when
W
is a space-time white noise, we have

(3.60)

\mathbb{E}[|u_{n}(z,t)|^{p}]^{1/p}\leq\mathchoice{\frac{\left(C(p-1)\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}z^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}}{\left(C(p-1)\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}z^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}{\frac{\left(C(p-1)\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}z^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}}{\frac{\left(C(p-1)\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}z^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}}.

Moreover, for every
z,t>0
and
p\geq 2
, we have
u(z,t)\in L^{p}(\Omega)
and the series in (
3.4
) converges to
u(z,t)
in
L^{p}(\Omega)
.

Proof.

We first revisit the case
p=2
.
For
n\geq 1
and
\beta\geq 1/4
, we show that for
z,t>0
,

(3.61)

\mathbb{E}[u_{n}^{2}(z,t)]\leq\mathchoice{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}.

We proceed by induction on
n
.
The base
n=1
follows from Lemma
3.7
.
Now assume that (
3.61
) holds for
n-1
with
n\geq 2
.
Using (
3.38
) with (formally)
\gamma=f=\delta_{0}
together with (
3.57
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n+1}^{2}\left(z,t\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n}^{2}\left(w,\tau\right)\right]dwd\tau

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}dw\tau^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}d\tau

\displaystyle(\beta>1/4)

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{C^{n}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)dw\tau^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(t-\tau\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\tau^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}d\tau

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathchoice{\frac{\sqrt{\pi}\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}}{\sqrt{\pi}\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{\sqrt{\pi}\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{\sqrt{\pi}\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}}t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}}{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{C^{n+1}\pi^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}+1\right)}}z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}}.

Therefore (
3.61
) holds for all
n\geq 1
.
Furthermore, it follows from a simple convergence test that the series

(3.62)

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[u_{n}^{2}(z,t)]\leq u_{0}^{2}(z,t)+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{\sqrt{z}}}{1/\sqrt{z}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{z}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{z}}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{(C\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t})^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{(C\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t})^{n}/\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}{\frac{(C\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t})^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}{\frac{(C\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{t})^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}+1\right)}}=u_{0}^{2}(z,t)+z^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\phi(C\sqrt{\pi t})

converges for any
z,t>0
, where

(3.63)

\phi(x)=e^{x^{2}}\left(1+\mathchoice{\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}}{2/\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}}\int_{0}^{x}e^{-s^{2}}\mathrm{d}s\right).

Continuing to general
p\geq 2
, (
3.60
) follows immediately by (
2.5
) and (
3.61
).
Again, by a convergence test, for every
z,t>0
,

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\mathbb{E}[|u_{n}(z,t)^{p}|]\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}<\infty,

which implies that
u(z,t)\in L^{p}(\Omega)
and that
u(z,t)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}u_{n}(z,t)
in
L^{p}(\Omega)
.
∎

3.3.2.
Colored Noise

With
\mathsection\ref{ssec:momentSTWN}
and
\mathsection\ref{ssec:euColored}
, we almost have all the necessary tools to prove a version of Theorem
3.5
corresponding to colored noise kernels
\gamma
and
f
.
Notice that by (
2.5
) and (
3.36
),

(3.64)

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}[|u_{n}(z,t)|^{p}]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}\leq u_{0}(z,t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{\sqrt{p-1}\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\sqrt{p-1}\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}/2}{\frac{\sqrt{p-1}\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}{\frac{\sqrt{p-1}\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}}{2}}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}\left(1+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{1/(k+1)!}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}{\frac{1}{(k+1)!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{2f(\varepsilon)t/F_{\varepsilon}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}{\frac{2f(\varepsilon)t}{F_{\varepsilon}}}\right)^{k+1}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}.

The only problem with convergence stems from the geometric series, while the rest converges for some
\varepsilon>0
sufficiently small, depending on
z,t>0
and
p\geq 2
.

Recall that this bound was obtained by recursively applying in (
3.38
) the uniform bound in Lemma
3.2
, of which we now have the refined version (
3.58
).
Hence, we can now incorporate (
3.58
) to prove convergence of (
3.64
).
In addition, we can show that it is possible to obtain convergence for all
z,t>0
, independent of the choice of
\varepsilon>0
.

Theorem 3.6
.

For every
n\geq 1
,
p\geq 2
,
\beta\geq 1/4
, and
z,t>0
, if
f
satisfies conditions (
i
) and (
ii
), then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|u_{n}\left(z,t\right)\right|^{p}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}

\displaystyle\leq\hat{z}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}}{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}}{\frac{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}}

(3.65)

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\cdot\left[1+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Finally, for every
z,t>0
we have
u(z,t)\in L^{p}(\Omega)
and the series in (
3.4
) converges to
u(z,t)
in
L^{p}(\Omega)
.

Proof.

Let
z_{1},z_{2},t_{1},t_{2},\epsilon>0
. Note that

\max\left(\left(z_{1}z_{2}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}},1\right)\leq\left(\hat{z_{1}}\hat{z_{2}}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}.

We claim that for every
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[u_{n}\left(z_{1},t_{1}\right)u_{n}\left(z_{2},t_{2}\right)\right]\leq\left(\hat{z_{1}}\hat{z_{2}}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\cdot\left[\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{4}}{n/4}{\frac{n}{4}}{\frac{n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right].

First, we examine the base case when
n=1
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{1}\left(z_{1},t_{1}\right)u_{1}\left(z_{2},t_{2}\right)\right]\leq\int_{\left[0,t_{1}\right]\times\left[0,t_{2}\right]}\hskip-39.83385pt\gamma\left(r-s\right)\left(\int_{A_{\epsilon}}+\int_{A_{\epsilon}^{\complement}}\right)f\left(x-y\right)\hat{x}^{\beta}y^{\beta}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\cdot q_{0}(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r)q_{0}(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s)\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n-1}(x,r)u_{n-1}(y,s)\right]dxdydrds

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{x}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)dxdr\int_{0}^{t_{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{y}^{2\beta}q_{0}^{2}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)dyds}

\displaystyle+f\left(\epsilon\right)\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{t_{1}}\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{x}^{\beta}q_{0}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)dx\right|^{2}\hskip-5.69054ptdr\int_{0}^{t_{2}}\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{y}^{\beta}q_{0}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)dy\right|^{2}ds}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}\mathchoice{\frac{C\sqrt{\pi}}{\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}}{C\sqrt{\pi}/\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}{\frac{C\sqrt{\pi}}{\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}}{\frac{C\sqrt{\pi}}{\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}}\left(z_{1}z_{2}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}+\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t_{1}t_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\left(\hat{z_{1}}\hat{z_{2}}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\left[\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(3/2\right)}}+\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(2\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(2\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(2\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(2\right)}}\right],

which matches the desired expression. Now assume that the expression
holds for
n-1
for some
n\geq 2
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n}\left(z_{1},t_{1}\right)u_{n}\left(z_{2},t_{2}\right)\right]=\int_{\left[0,t_{1}\right]\times\left[0,t_{2}\right]}\hskip-39.83385pt\gamma\left(r-s\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\hskip-9.95845ptf\left(x-y\right)

\displaystyle\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}q_{0}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)q_{0}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n-1}\left(x,r\right)u_{n-1}\left(y,s\right)\right]dxdydrds.

Similarly, we split the integral above in
\left(x,y\right)
into

A_{\epsilon}
and
A_{\epsilon}^{\complement}
, and treat each
term separately.
First, denoting

(3.66)

U_{n}(z,t):=\int_{0}^{t}r^{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}(z,x,t-r)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}r.

Then, by the induction assumption and (
3.57
),

\displaystyle\int_{\left[0,t_{1}\right]\times\left[0,t_{2}\right]}\hskip-39.83385pt\gamma\left(r-s\right)\int_{A_{\epsilon}}\hskip-9.95845ptf\left(x-y\right)\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}q_{0}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)q_{0}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n-1}\left(x,r\right)u_{n-1}\left(y,s\right)\right]dxdydrds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n-1}\int_{\left[0,t_{1}\right]\times\left[0,t_{2}\right]}\hskip-34.1433pt\gamma\left(r-s\right)\int_{A_{\epsilon}}f\left(x-y\right)\hat{x}^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\hat{y}^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}q_{0}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)q_{0}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left[\mathchoice{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}+\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{4}}{n+k/4}{\frac{n+k}{4}}{\frac{n+k}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{4}}{n+k/4}{\frac{n+k}{4}}{\frac{n+k}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{4}}{n+k/4}{\frac{n+k}{4}}{\frac{n+k}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{4}}{n+k/4}{\frac{n+k}{4}}{\frac{n+k}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}\right]dxdydrds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n-1}\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}\right)^{n}\left\{\mathchoice{\frac{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{1},t_{1})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(U_{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}(z_{2},t_{2})\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n}\left(\hat{z_{1}}\hat{z_{2}}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\left[\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{4}}{n/4}{\frac{n}{4}}{\frac{n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{4}}{n/4}{\frac{n}{4}}{\frac{n}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{4}}{n/4}{\frac{n}{4}}{\frac{n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{4}}{n/4}{\frac{n}{4}}{\frac{n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}+\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{n+k+1/4}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{n+k+1/4}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{n+k+1/4}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{n+k+1/4}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}{\frac{n+k+1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right].

Next, again by the induction assumption,

\displaystyle\int_{\left[0,t_{1}\right]\times\left[0,t_{2}\right]}\hskip-39.83385pt\gamma\left(r-s\right)\int_{A_{\epsilon}^{\complement}}\hskip-9.95845ptf\left(x-y\right)\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}q_{0}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)q_{0}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n-1}\left(x,r\right)u_{n-1}\left(y,s\right)\right]dxdydrds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n-1}\int_{\left[0,t_{1}\right]\times\left[0,t_{2}\right]}\hskip-39.83385pt\gamma\left(r-s\right)\int_{A_{\epsilon}^{\complement}}\hskip-9.95845ptf\left(x-y\right)\hat{x}^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\hat{y}^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}q_{0}\left(z_{1},x,t_{1}-r\right)q_{0}\left(z_{2},y,t_{2}-s\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\left[\mathchoice{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{4}}{n-1/4}{\frac{n-1}{4}}{\frac{n-1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}+\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{k+n}{4}}{k+n/4}{\frac{k+n}{4}}{\frac{k+n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{k+n}{4}}{k+n/4}{\frac{k+n}{4}}{\frac{k+n}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{k+n}{4}}{k+n/4}{\frac{k+n}{4}}{\frac{k+n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(rs\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{k+n}{4}}{k+n/4}{\frac{k+n}{4}}{\frac{k+n}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}\right]dxdydrds

\displaystyle\leq
