\displaystyle\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n}\left\{\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n-1}{2}+1\right)}}+\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+2}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(\int_{0}^{t_{1}}r^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}dr\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{2}}s^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k}{2}}{n+k/2}{\frac{n+k}{2}}{\frac{n+k}{2}}}ds\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k}{2}+1\right)}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\Gamma_{t_{1}\vee t_{2}}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n}\left(\hat{z_{1}}\hat{z_{2}}\right)^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\left[\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{4}}{n+1/4}{\frac{n+1}{4}}{\frac{n+1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{4}}{n+1/4}{\frac{n+1}{4}}{\frac{n+1}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{4}}{n+1/4}{\frac{n+1}{4}}{\frac{n+1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{4}}{n+1/4}{\frac{n+1}{4}}{\frac{n+1}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 110.96574pt+\left.\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+2}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{n+k+2/4}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+2}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{n+k+2/4}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+2}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{n+k+2/4}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+2}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(t_{1}t_{2}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{n+k+2/4}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}{\frac{n+k+2}{4}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+2}{2}+1\right)}}\right].

Adding the two expressions above and regrouping terms in the summation,
we confirm that
\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n}\left(z_{1},t_{1}\right)u_{n}\left(z_{2},t_{2}\right)\right]

takes the desired form.

Moreover, we get that for every
z,t>0
and
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[u_{n}^{2}\left(z,t\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\hat{z}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}\right)^{n}\cdot

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left[\mathchoice{\frac{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{n+k+1/2}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{n+k+1/2}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{n+k+1/2}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{t^{\mathchoice{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{n+k+1/2}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}{\frac{n+k+1}{2}}}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\hat{z}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathchoice{\frac{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{n}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}\left[1+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right],

and hence for all
p\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|u_{n}\left(z,t\right)\right|^{p}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}\leq\hat{z}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}}{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}/\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}}{\frac{\left(\left(p-1\right)\Gamma_{t}F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}}

\displaystyle\cdot\left[1+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}/F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}{\frac{f\left(\epsilon\right)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\epsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since

\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\leq\mathchoice{\frac{2}{\Gamma\left(\frac{k+1}{2}\right)}}{2/\Gamma\left(\frac{k+1}{2}\right)}{\frac{2}{\Gamma\left(\frac{k+1}{2}\right)}}{\frac{2}{\Gamma\left(\frac{k+1}{2}\right)}},

it is straightforward to verify that for all
z,t>0
and
p\geq 2
,

\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|u_{n}\left(z,t\right)\right|^{p}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}<\infty.

∎

3.4.
Hölder Continuity up to Boundary

As mentioned in
\mathsection\ref{ssec:outline}
, now that we have bounds for
L^{p}-
norms for
p\geq 2
and refined bounds for
L^{2}
norms, we continue to establish Hölder continuity of the solution to (
3.1
).
For
z_{1},z_{2},t_{1},t_{2}>0
, similarly to the derivation of (
3.5
), the process
u(z_{1},t_{1})-u(z_{2},t_{2})\in L^{2}(\Omega)
and admits the following Wiener chaos expansion

(3.67)

\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u(z_1, t_1) - u(z_2, t_2)}^{2}\right]=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{I}_{n}\left(f_{n}(\cdot;z_{1},z_{2},t_{1},t_{2})\right),

where we abuse the notation to denote
f_{0}(z_{1},z_{2},t_{1},t_{2}):=u_{0}(z_{1},t_{1})-u_{0}(z_{2},t_{2})
and

(3.68)

f_{n}(\cdot;z_{1},z_{2},t_{1},t_{2}):=f_{n}(\cdot;z_{1},t_{1})-f_{n}(\cdot;z_{2},t_{2})

for
f_{n}(\cdot;z_{1},t_{1})
and
f_{n}(\cdot;z_{2},t_{2})
as in (
3.5
).
Hence, setting

(3.69)

d_{0}(z_{1},z_{2},w,t):=q_{0}(z_{1},w,t)-q_{0}(z_{1},w,t),

for
z_{1},z_{2},w,t>0
, then for
n\geq 1
and
t_{1}=t_{2}=t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{2}\right]=\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\hskip-17.07164pt\gamma(r-s)\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581ptf(x-y)\hat{x}^{\beta}\hat{y}^{\beta}d_{0}(z_{1},z_{2},x,t-r)d_{0}(z_{1},z_{2},y,t-s)

(3.70)

\displaystyle\cdot\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}(x,r)u_{n-1}(y,s)]\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s.

Thus we can reuse the refined bounds developed in
\mathsection\ref{sec:moment}
.
We shall show that for
\beta>1/4
, the map
z\mapsto u(z,t)
is Hölder continuous in
L^{p}(\Omega)
for any fixed
t>0
.

3.4.1.
Space-Time White Noise

In order to show the Hölder continuity, we seek to bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{2}\right]
by a power of
|z_{1}-z_{2}|
.
We begin by first examining the case of space-time white noise.
In this case, (
3.70
) becomes

(3.71)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{2}\right]=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}d_{0}^{2}(z_{1},z_{2},w,t-\tau)\mathbb{E}[u_{n-1}^{2}(w,\tau)]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau,

In light of (
3.61
), for
n\geq 2
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{2}\right]\leq\mathchoice{\frac{(C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n-1}}{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}{(C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n-1}/\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}{\frac{(C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n-1}}{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}{\frac{(C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n-1}}{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}d_{0}^{2}(z_{1},z_{2},w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau.

So, no recursive argument is needed.
Instead, we need only focus on bounding

(3.72)

Q_{t}(z_{1},z_{2}):=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}d_{0}^{2}(z_{1},z_{2},w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau.

To this end, we first present some technical results on the Hölder continuity of the map
z\mapsto q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)
for
w,t-\tau,\tau>0
.

Lemma 3.8
.

There exists a constant
M>0
such that for every
z_{1},z_{2},w,t-\tau,\tau>0
,

(3.73)

\absolutevalue{d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau)}\leq M\mathchoice{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}}{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}}.

Proof.

By direct computation, we have that

\partial_{z}q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)=\mathchoice{\frac{1}{t-\tau}}{1/t-\tau}{\frac{1}{t-\tau}}{\frac{1}{t-\tau}}\left(q_{1}(z,w,t-\tau)-q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)\right),

where
q_{1}
is given by (
2.17
), with
\nu=1
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\absolutevalue{d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau)}

\displaystyle=\absolutevalue{\int_{z_1}^{z_2} \partial_z q_0(z, w, t-\tau) \mathrm dz}

\displaystyle=\absolutevalue{\frac{1}{t-\tau}\int_{z_1}^{z_2} q_1(z, w, t) - q_0(z,w,t) \mathrm dz}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/t-\tau}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}\left[\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(q_{1}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)+q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\right)\mathrm{d}z\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Again, by direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{1}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}}{2(t-\tau)}}{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}/2(t-\tau)}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}}{2(t-\tau)}}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}}{2(t-\tau)}}{}_{2}F_{2}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}},1\\
1,1\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Bigg|}\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}\right],

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}}{4(t-\tau)}}{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}/4(t-\tau)}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}}{4(t-\tau)}}{\frac{e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}}}{4(t-\tau)}}{}_{2}F_{2}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}},3\\
2,3\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Bigg|}\mathchoice{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{2w/t-\tau}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2w}{t-\tau}}\right].

Combining these last two computations with Proposition
5.4
yields

\absolutevalue{d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau)}\leq\mathchoice{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/t-\tau}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{t-\tau}}\cdot\mathchoice{\frac{M}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}{M/\sqrt{t-\tau}}{\frac{M}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}{\frac{M}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}}=M\mathchoice{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}}{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}}{\frac{|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}}}},

as claimed.
∎

Therefore, we observe that it is possible to extrapolate the Hölder continuity of the stochastic solution from the Hölder continuity of the fundamental solution to the deterministic, unperturbed problem.
It thus remains to treat the aftermath of substituting (
3.73
) into (
3.72
) in the hopes of obtaining a bound independent of
z_{1}
and
z_{2}
.

Lemma 3.9
.

Fix
\beta>1/4
and
t>0
.
For
z_{1},z_{2}>0
and any
0\leq\lambda<(4\beta-1)\wedge 1/2
, there exists a constant
M_{\beta,\lambda}>0
such that

(3.74)

Q_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})\leq M_{\beta,\lambda}t^{\eta}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}},

where

(3.75)

\eta=\left[\left(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}\right]-\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}

Proof.

By Lemma
3.8
, for any
\lambda\in(0,2)
, we have

\displaystyle Q_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\leq M_{\lambda}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta}w^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\absolutevalue{d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau)}^{2-\lambda}\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau,

\displaystyle\leq M_{\lambda}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.53581pt\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\hskip-4.2679pt\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hskip-4.2679pt\hat{w}^{2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[q_{0}^{2-\lambda}(z_{1},w,t-\tau)+q_{0}^{2-\lambda}(z_{2},w,t-\tau)\right]\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau.

It remains to treat, for any
z>0
,

(3.76)

Q:=\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2-\lambda}(z,w,t-\tau)\hat{w}^{2\beta-1/2}\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau.

Note that
\hat{w}^{2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\hat{w}^{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\leq w^{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}
.
We apply the monotonicity of the moments to bound (
3.76
) from above as

\displaystyle Q

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}w^{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}q_{0}^{2-\lambda}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}w^{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}q_{0}^{1-\lambda}(z,w,t-\tau)(q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w)\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{\mathchoice{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{3\lambda/2}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}{\frac{3\lambda}{2}}}}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}w^{\mathchoice{\frac{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-\lambda}}{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}/1-\lambda}{\frac{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-\lambda}}{\frac{(2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}{1-\lambda}}}q_{0}^{2}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\right)^{1-\lambda}\mathrm{d}\tau.

Set
\xi:=\mathchoice{\frac{((2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}})}{(1-\lambda)}}{((2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}})/(1-\lambda)}{\frac{((2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}})}{(1-\lambda)}}{\frac{((2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}})}{(1-\lambda)}}
.
Then, by Proposition
5.4
, the integral inside the power above can be bounded as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}^{2}\left(z,w,t-\tau\right)w^{\xi}dw

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma\left(1+\xi\right)}{2^{3+\xi}}}{\Gamma\left(1+\xi\right)/2^{3+\xi}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(1+\xi\right)}{2^{3+\xi}}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(1+\xi\right)}{2^{3+\xi}}}\left(t-\tau\right)^{\xi-1}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{2z/t-\tau}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}\right)^{2}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{2z/t-\tau}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}}{}_{2}F_{2}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}},\xi+1\\
2,3\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Bigg|}\mathchoice{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{2z/t-\tau}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{2z}{t-\tau}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq M_{\beta,\lambda}\left(t-\tau\right)^{\xi-1},

provided that
\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}+\xi+1-2-3\leq-2
, i.e., when

(3.77)

\lambda\leq\begin{cases}2-4\beta&\text{ if }\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}<\beta\leq\mathchoice{\frac{3}{8}}{3/8}{\frac{3}{8}}{\frac{3}{8}},\\
\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}&\text{ if }\beta>\mathchoice{\frac{3}{8}}{3/8}{\frac{3}{8}}{\frac{3}{8}}.\end{cases}

It follows from above that

Q_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})\leq M_{\beta,\lambda}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}}\int_{0}^{t}(t-\tau)^{(\xi-1)(1-\lambda)-\mathchoice{\frac{3}{2}}{3/2}{\frac{3}{2}}{\frac{3}{2}}\lambda}\mathrm{d}\tau=M_{\beta,\lambda}t^{\eta}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}}.

Note that by taking
0\leq\lambda<(4\beta-1)\wedge 1/2
, we always have
\eta>0
and (
3.77
).
∎

We are finally equipped to present the main result of this section.

Theorem 3.7
.

Fix
\beta>1/4
and
t>0
.
Then, for any
Z>0
and any
\theta\in(0,(\beta-1/4)\wedge 1/8)
, there exists an a.s.
\theta-
Hölder continuous modification of the process
z\in[0,Z]\mapsto u(z,t)\in\mathbb{R}
.
In particular,
z\mapsto u(z,t)
is continuous all the way to the boundary 0.

Proof.

For any
p\geq 2
and
n\geq 1
, by (
2.5
) and Lemma
3.9
, for some constant
M_{p,\beta,\gamma}>0
,

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|u_{n}(z_{1},t)-u_{n}(z_{2},t)|^{p}\right]\right)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\leq(p-1)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|u_{n}(z_{1},t)-u_{n}(z_{2},t)|^{2}\right]\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}},

(3.78)

\displaystyle\leq M_{p,\beta,\lambda}\mathchoice{\frac{\left((p-1)C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}}{\left((p-1)C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}/\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}{\frac{\left((p-1)C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}}{\frac{\left((p-1)C\sqrt{\pi t}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n-1}{2}}{n-1/2}{\frac{n-1}{2}}{\frac{n-1}{2}}}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}}t^{\mathchoice{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\eta/2}{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\frac{\eta}{2}}}.

Substituting (
3.78
) into (
3.67
),

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|u(z_{1},t)-u(z_{2},t)|^{p}\right]\right)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt\leq\absolutevalue{u_0(z_1,t) - u_0(z_2, t)}+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}M_{p,\beta,\lambda}\mathchoice{\frac{(C\sqrt{t})^{n-1}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}}{(C\sqrt{t})^{n-1}/\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}{\frac{(C\sqrt{t})^{n-1}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}}{\frac{(C\sqrt{t})^{n-1}}{\sqrt{\Gamma\left(\displaystyle\mathchoice{\frac{n+1}{2}}{n+1/2}{\frac{n+1}{2}}{\frac{n+1}{2}}\right)}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\lambda/4}t^{\eta/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt=\absolutevalue{e^{-\frac{z_1}{t}} - e^{-\frac{z_2}{t}}}+M_{p,\beta,\lambda,t}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}}.

When
p
is sufficiently large, Kolmogorov’s continuity theorem implies that for every
t>0
,
z\mapsto u(z,t)
is almost surely
\theta
-Hölder continuous for any
\theta\in\left(0,\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}\right)
.
Since
p
can be arbitrarily large and
\lambda
can be arbitrarily close to
(4\beta-1)\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}
, we conclude that for every
t>0
,
z\mapsto u(z,t)
is almost surely
\theta
-Hölder continuous for any
\theta\in(0,(\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{8}}{1/8}{\frac{1}{8}}{\frac{1}{8}})
all the way to the boundary 0.
∎

3.4.2.
Colored Noise

As before, we seek to apply the usual approach.
Recall from
\mathsection\ref{ssec:momentColored}
that for
x,y>0
,
r,s\in(0,t)
, and any
n\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[u_{n}(x,r)u_{n}(y,s)]\leq\hat{x}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}

\displaystyle\hat{y}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\mathchoice{\frac{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n}/\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{(\Gamma_{t}F_{\varepsilon}C\sqrt{\pi t})^{n}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}}

(3.79)

\displaystyle\cdot\left[1+\sum_{m=1}^{n}\sum_{k=0}^{m-1}\binom{m-1}{k}\left(\frac{f(\varepsilon)\sqrt{t}}{F_{\varepsilon}C\sqrt{\pi}}\right)^{k+1}\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)/\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}{\frac{\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}+1\right)}{\Gamma\left(\frac{n+k+1}{2}+1\right)}}\right]

which forms a convergent series for
\varepsilon>0
sufficiently small.
Let
M_{n,\varepsilon,t}
denote the right hand side of (
3.79
) excluding the factor
\hat{x}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\hat{y}^{-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}
.
Similarly to the previous section, from (
3.70
) and Proposition
5.2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{2}\right]\leq M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}\int_{[0,t]^{2}}\hskip-17.07164pt\gamma(r-s)\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\hskip-8.53581ptf(x-y)\hat{x}^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\hat{y}^{\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\cdot d_{0}(z_{1},z_{2},x,t-r)d_{0}(z_{1},z_{2},y,t-s)\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}s,

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{t}M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}\left(F_{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\infty}\hat{w}^{2\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}d_{0}^{2}(z_{1},z_{2},w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}\tau\right.

(3.80)

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 85.35826pt+f(\varepsilon)\int_{0}^{t}\absolutevalue{\int_0^\infty\hat w^{\beta-\frac{1}{4}} d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau)\mathrm dw}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau\right).

Again, no recursive argument is needed.
Notice that the first integral in the right hand side of (
3.80
) is bounded by
Q_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})
from the previous section.
However, we need also extract a power of
|z_{1}-z_{2}|
from the second integral.
The following result shows that we can obtain an exponent which is no worse for Hölder continuity than the one seen in the previous section.

Lemma 3.10
.

Fix
\beta>1/4
and
t>0
.
For
z_{1},z_{2}>0
, set

(3.81)

\tilde{Q}_{t}(z_{1},z_{2}):=\int_{0}^{t}\absolutevalue{\int_0^\infty\hat w^{\beta-\frac{1}{4}} d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau) \mathrm dw}^{2}\mathrm{d}\tau.

Then, there exists a constant
M>0
such that for any
z_{1},z_{2}>0
,

(3.82)

\tilde{Q}_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})\leq Mt^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{4}}{3/4}{\frac{3}{4}}{\frac{3}{4}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}.

Proof.

It’s clear that

(3.83)

\absolutevalue{\int_0^\infty\hat w^{\beta- \frac{1}{4}}d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau)\mathrm dw }\leq 2.

Choose
p\in(0,2)
.
By (
3.83
), we have

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2-p}\int_{0}^{t}\absolutevalue{\int_0^\infty\hat w^{\beta- \frac{1}{4}} d_0(z_1,z_2,w,t-\tau) \mathrm dw}^{p}\mathrm{d}\tau,

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2-p}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{p}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}\left(\int_{z_{1}}^{z_{2}}q_{1}(z,w,t-\tau)+q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}z\right)^{p}\mathrm{d}\tau.

By direct computation, we observe

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{1}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{1}{t-\tau}}{1/t-\tau}{\frac{1}{t-\tau}}{\frac{1}{t-\tau}}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{z/t-\tau}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{z^{n}}{(t-\tau)^{2n}(n!)^{2}}}{z^{n}/(t-\tau)^{2n}(n!)^{2}}{\frac{z^{n}}{(t-\tau)^{2n}(n!)^{2}}}{\frac{z^{n}}{(t-\tau)^{2n}(n!)^{2}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{w}{t-\tau}}{w/t-\tau}{\frac{w}{t-\tau}}{\frac{w}{t-\tau}}}w^{n}\mathrm{d}w

\displaystyle=e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{z/t-\tau}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{n!}}{1/n!}{\frac{1}{n!}}{\frac{1}{n!}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{z/t-\tau}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}{\frac{z}{t-\tau}}\right)^{n}=1.

Therefore together with Lemma
3.1
,

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{t}(z_{1},z_{2})

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2-p}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{p}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}\left(\int_{z_{1}}^{z_{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{1}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w+\int_{0}^{\infty}q_{0}(z,w,t-\tau)\mathrm{d}w\mathrm{d}z\right)^{p}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\leq 4|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{p}\int_{0}^{t}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}{1/(t-\tau)^{p}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}{\frac{1}{(t-\tau)^{p}}}\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{4}{1-p}}{4/1-p}{\frac{4}{1-p}}{\frac{4}{1-p}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{p}t^{1-p}.

Taking
p=\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}
, we obtain the desired bound.
∎

Theorem 3.8
.

Fix
\beta>\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}
and
t>0
.
Then, for any
Z>0
and
\theta\in(\beta-1/4)\wedge 1/8
, there exists an a.s.
\theta
-Hölder continuous modification of the process
z\in[0,Z]\mapsto u(z,t)\in\mathbb{R}
.
In particular,
z\in u(z,t)
is continuous all the way to the boundary.

Proof.

For any
n\geq 1
and
p=2
, by substituting (
3.74
) and (
3.82
) into (
3.80
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{2}\right]\leq\Gamma_{t}M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}\left(M_{\beta,\lambda}F_{\varepsilon}t^{\eta}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}}+Mf(\varepsilon)t^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{4}}{3/4}{\frac{3}{4}}{\frac{3}{4}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\right),

for
\eta
and
\lambda
as in Lemma
3.9
.
Hence for
p\geq 2
, by (
2.5
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\absolutevalue{u_n(z_1, t) - u_n(z_2, t)}^{p}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sqrt{\Gamma_{t}M_{\beta,\lambda}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))}(p-1)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}\sqrt{M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}}\left(t^{\eta}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\lambda/2}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}{\frac{\lambda}{2}}}+t^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{4}}{3/4}{\frac{3}{4}}{\frac{3}{4}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}}

(3.84)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sqrt{\Gamma_{t}M_{\beta,\lambda}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))}(p-1)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}\sqrt{M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}}\left(t^{\mathchoice{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\eta/2}{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\frac{\eta}{2}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}}+t^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{8}}{3/8}{\frac{3}{8}}{\frac{3}{8}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{8}}{1/8}{\frac{1}{8}}{\frac{1}{8}}}\right),

where
(p-1)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}\sqrt{M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}}
is summable over
n
, independent of the values of the other variables.
Substituting (
3.84
) into (
3.67
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\absolutevalue{u(z_1, t) - u(z_2, t)}^{p}\right]^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}\leq|u_{0}(z_{1},t)-u_{0}(z_{2},t)|

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\Gamma_{t}M_{\beta,\lambda}(F_{\varepsilon}+f(\varepsilon))}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}(p-1)^{\mathchoice{\frac{n}{2}}{n/2}{\frac{n}{2}}{\frac{n}{2}}}\sqrt{M_{n-1,\varepsilon,t}}\left(t^{\mathchoice{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\eta/2}{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\frac{\eta}{2}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}}+t^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{8}}{3/8}{\frac{3}{8}}{\frac{3}{8}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{8}}{1/8}{\frac{1}{8}}{\frac{1}{8}}}\right),

\displaystyle=|e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{z_{1}}{t}}{z_{1}/t}{\frac{z_{1}}{t}}{\frac{z_{1}}{t}}}-e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{z_{2}}{t}}{z_{2}/t}{\frac{z_{2}}{t}}{\frac{z_{2}}{t}}}|+M_{t,\varepsilon,\beta,\lambda}\left(t^{\mathchoice{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\eta/2}{\frac{\eta}{2}}{\frac{\eta}{2}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}}+t^{\mathchoice{\frac{3}{8}}{3/8}{\frac{3}{8}}{\frac{3}{8}}}|z_{1}-z_{2}|^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{8}}{1/8}{\frac{1}{8}}{\frac{1}{8}}}\right).

When
p
is sufficiently large, Kolmogorov’s continuity theorem implies that for every
t>0
,
z\mapsto u(z,t)
is almost surely
\theta
-Hölder continuous for any
\theta\in\left(0,\mathchoice{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\lambda/4}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}{\frac{\lambda}{4}}-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}\right)
.
Since
p
can be arbitrarily large and
\lambda
can be arbitrarily close to
(4\beta-1)\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}
, we conclude that for every
t>0
,
z\mapsto u(z,t)
is almost surely
\theta
-Hölder continuous for any
\theta\in(0,(\beta-\mathchoice{\frac{1}{4}}{1/4}{\frac{1}{4}}{\frac{1}{4}})\wedge\mathchoice{\frac{1}{8}}{1/8}{\frac{1}{8}}{\frac{1}{8}})
.
∎

4.
Conclusion and Further Questions

Beyond the results demonstrated above for existence, uniqueness, estimates, and continuity of the solution to (
1.6
), we are interested in exploring other commonly studied properties of SPDEs, such as long-term asymptotics and intermittency (see
[
11
,
8
,
25
,
33
,
10
]
).

Moreover, while we have demonstrated results in the special case of a driftless (
\nu=0
) stochastic Kimura equation, the deterministic problem (
2.16
) studied in
[
14
]
possesses a more general constant drift,
\nu<1
.
At present, we have no cause to believe that the introduction of such a drift term would have a qualitative impact on existence, uniqueness, estimates or continuity of the solution.
This is because the only change that would occur is to replace
q_{0}(z,w,t)
by
q_{\nu}(z,w,t)
.
We can follow exactly the same steps explained in
\mathsection\ref{sec:results}
to construct and to study the solution to the stochastic Kimura equation with a constant drift
\nu<1
.
We expect the same techniques employed in the above to work to remain applicable, albeit more involved to apply.
These technical challenges stem from the fact that computations such as (
3.6
) and (
3.9
) are special cases of formulas involving hypergeometric functions.

On the other hand,
it is not clear whether the estimates presented in this work, particularly the integral bounds derived in
\mathsection\ref{sec:results}
, are all sharp.
This is in part due to the spatial integrals over the domain
\mathbb{R}_{+}
, for which we use estimates for integrands involving local degeneracies
\hat{z}
with Bessel functions or hypergeometric functions.
Hence, employing tighter bounds in these steps would yield tighter bounds overall.
In the same vein, it remains to be examined whether the requirement on the order of the degeneracy
\hat{z}
of the noise near the boundary can be relaxed for obtaining the estimates and continuity in
\mathsection\ref{sec:moment}
and
\mathsection\ref{sec:cont}
.
In light of improving sharpness overall in the results, it may be possible recover the same results under less degenerate or even non-degenerate Gaussian noise using more refined estimates.

Finally, in the deterministic setting, the Wright-Fisher equation, which is the two-sided boundary analog of the Kimura equation, can be transformed into a one-sided boundary model through a localization method
[
13
]
.
It would be interesting to explore and to adapt the localization method in the stochastic setting, which may allow us to investigate the solution to the stochastic Wright-Fisher equation and compare
it to the solution of the stochastic Kimura equation studied in this work.

5.
Appendix

In this section, we collect some inequalities and identities that are used in
\mathsection\ref{sec:results}
.
We omit all the proofs.

Proposition 5.1
.

(Moment Estimate of Gaussian Distribution)

For every
p>-1
there exists a constant
C_{p}>0
such that

(5.1)

\mathchoice{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^{2}}}}{1/\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^{2}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^{2}}}}{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^{2}}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}|u|^{p}e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{(u-m)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}}{(u-m)^{2}/2\sigma^{2}}{\frac{(u-m)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}}{\frac{(u-m)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}}}\mathrm{d}u\leq C_{p}(m^{p}+\sigma^{p}).

Proposition 5.2
.

(Young’s Convolution Inequality, Theorem 3.9.4 in
[
9
]
)

Consider some sets
A,B,C\subseteq\mathbb{R}
and locally integrable functions
f,g,h:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}
.
Denote
D=\left\{(x,y)\in B\times C|x-y\in A\right\}
, then for all
p,q,r\geq 1
such that

\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{q}}{1/q}{\frac{1}{q}}{\frac{1}{q}}+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{r}}{1/r}{\frac{1}{r}}{\frac{1}{r}}=2,

(5.2)

\absolutevalue{\int_D f(x-y)g(x)h(y)\mathrm dx \mathrm dy}\leq\left(\int_{A}|f|^{r}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{r}}{1/r}{\frac{1}{r}}{\frac{1}{r}}}\left(\int_{B}|g|^{p}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{p}}{1/p}{\frac{1}{p}}{\frac{1}{p}}}\left(\int_{C}|h|^{q}\right)^{\mathchoice{\frac{1}{q}}{1/q}{\frac{1}{q}}{\frac{1}{q}}}.

Next, we present various special functions.
For more details, see
[
38
]
.

Bessel Functions
.
Let
\alpha\in\mathbb{C}
, the
Bessel function of the first kind

J_{\alpha}
are defined as the solution to

x^{2}\derivative[2]{y}{x}+x\derivative{y}{x}+(x^{2}-\alpha^{2})y=0,

which is non-singular at the origin.
They have various representations, for example,

J_{\alpha}(x)=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{(-1)^{m}}{m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}}{(-1)^{m}/m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}{\frac{(-1)^{m}}{m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}}{\frac{(-1)^{m}}{m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{x}{2}}{x/2}{\frac{x}{2}}{\frac{x}{2}}\right)^{2m+\alpha}.

The
modified Bessel function of the first kind

I_{\alpha}
is given by

(5.3)

I_{\alpha}(x)=i^{-\alpha}J_{\alpha}(ix)=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{1}{m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}}{1/m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}{\frac{1}{m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}}{\frac{1}{m!\Gamma(m+\alpha+1)}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{x}{2}}{x/2}{\frac{x}{2}}{\frac{x}{2}}\right)^{2m+\alpha}.

They also have various representations and recurrence relations, for example for
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\derivative{x}\left[x^{-n}I_{n}(x)\right]=x^{-n}I_{n+1}(x).

In particular for
n=0
,
I_{0}^{\prime}(x)=I_{1}(x)
.

Hypergeometric Functions.

For each
n\geq 0
and
a\in\mathbb{R}
, we define the
Pochhammer symbol
as

(a)_{n}:=\begin{cases}1&;\text{ if }n=0\\
a(a+1)(a+2)\cdots(a+n-1)&;\text{ if }n\geq 1\end{cases}.

Given
q\geq p\geq 0
, and
a_{1},\ldots,a_{p},b_{1},\ldots,b_{q}\in\mathbb{R}
, if none of
b_{1},\ldots,b_{q}
are non-positive integers, then for
x\in\mathbb{C}
, the
generalized Hypergeometric function

{}_{p}F_{q}
is defined as

{}_{p}F_{q}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}a_{1},\ldots,a_{p}\\
b_{1},\ldots,b_{q}\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Big|}x\right]:=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\mathchoice{\frac{(a_{1})_{n}\cdots(a_{p})_{n}}{(b_{1})_{n}\cdots(b_{q})_{n}}}{(a_{1})_{n}\cdots(a_{p})_{n}/(b_{1})_{n}\cdots(b_{q})_{n}}{\frac{(a_{1})_{n}\cdots(a_{p})_{n}}{(b_{1})_{n}\cdots(b_{q})_{n}}}{\frac{(a_{1})_{n}\cdots(a_{p})_{n}}{(b_{1})_{n}\cdots(b_{q})_{n}}}\mathchoice{\frac{x^{n}}{n!}}{x^{n}/n!}{\frac{x^{n}}{n!}}{\frac{x^{n}}{n!}}.

Here are two technical results involving these functions:

Proposition 5.3
.

(
(13.6.5)
in
[
20
]
)

Let
a\in\mathbb{N}
, then

(5.4)

{}_{1}F_{1}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}1\\
a+1\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Big|}z\right]=ae^{z}z^{-a}\left(\Gamma(a)-\Gamma(a,z)\right)

Proposition 5.4
.

(Main Result in
[
37
]
)

Suppose that neither
c
nor
d
are non-positive integers, then
{}_{2}F_{2}
has the following asymptotic expansion:

(5.5)

\mathchoice{\frac{\Gamma(a)\Gamma(b)}{\Gamma(c)\Gamma(d)}}{\Gamma(a)\Gamma(b)/\Gamma(c)\Gamma(d)}{\frac{\Gamma(a)\Gamma(b)}{\Gamma(c)\Gamma(d)}}{\frac{\Gamma(a)\Gamma(b)}{\Gamma(c)\Gamma(d)}}{}_{2}F_{2}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}a,b\\
c,d\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Big|}z\right]\sim e^{z}z^{\nu}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}c_{k}z^{-k},

as
z\to\infty
, where
\nu=a+b-c-d
.
If

Proposition 5.5
.

Proposition 5.6
.

If
\real(\nu)>-1/2
,
\real(2\nu+\mu)>0
,
|\arg p|<\pi/4
, setting
\bar{\omega}=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}-2ab\cos\phi}
, then

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}}

\displaystyle\exp(-p^2u^2)J_{\nu}\left(au\right)J_{\nu}\left(bu\right)u^{\mu-1}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\frac{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\nu+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\mu\right)}{\displaystyle 2\pi p^{\mu}\Gamma\left(2\nu+1\right)}}{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\nu+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\mu\right)/\displaystyle 2\pi p^{\mu}\Gamma\left(2\nu+1\right)}{\frac{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\nu+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\mu\right)}{\displaystyle 2\pi p^{\mu}\Gamma\left(2\nu+1\right)}}{\frac{\displaystyle\Gamma\left(\nu+\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\mu\right)}{\displaystyle 2\pi p^{\mu}\Gamma\left(2\nu+1\right)}}\left(\mathchoice{\frac{ab}{p^{2}}}{ab/p^{2}}{\frac{ab}{p^{2}}}{\frac{ab}{p^{2}}}\right)^{\nu}\int_{0}^{\pi}\exp\left(-\mathchoice{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}{\bar{\omega}^{2}/4p^{2}}{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}\right){}_{1}F_{1}\left[\hskip-2.84526pt\begin{array}[]{cc}\displaystyle 1-\mathchoice{\frac{\mu}{2}}{\mu/2}{\frac{\mu}{2}}{\frac{\mu}{2}}\\
\nu+1\end{array}\hskip-5.69054pt\mathrel{\Bigg|}\mathchoice{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}{\bar{\omega}^{2}/4p^{2}}{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}\right]\sin^{2\nu}\phi\mathrm{d}\phi,

in which we are interested in the special case
\nu=1
,
\mu=0
, and
a=b>0
.
Thus
\bar{\omega}=\sqrt{2}a\sqrt{1-\cos{\phi}}
and

\mathchoice{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}{\bar{\omega}^{2}/4p^{2}}{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}{\frac{\bar{\omega}^{2}}{4p^{2}}}=\mathchoice{\frac{a^{2}(1-\cos\phi)}{2p^{2}}}{a^{2}(1-\cos\phi)/2p^{2}}{\frac{a^{2}(1-\cos\phi)}{2p^{2}}}{\frac{a^{2}(1-\cos\phi)}{2p^{2}}},

from which it follows

\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp(-p^2u^2)J_{1}\left(au\right)^{2}u^{-1}\mathrm{d}u=\mathchoice{\frac{1}{2}}{1/2}{\frac{1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1-e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{a^{2}/2p^{2}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}}I_{0}\left(-\mathchoice{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{a^{2}/2p^{2}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}\right)+e^{-\mathchoice{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{a^{2}/2p^{2}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}}I_{1}\left(-\mathchoice{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{a^{2}/2p^{2}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}{\frac{a^{2}}{2p^{2}}}\right)\right).
